--- START FILE: README.md ---
﻿# Helix: Enterprise Health Intelligence & Metabolic Decision Support OS

Helix is a source-grounded health intelligence platform that unifies multi-hospital diagnostic records, calculates biomarker velocity, simulates 90-day metabolic recovery trajectories, and generates HL7 FHIR-compliant clinical summaries.

## Core Features
- **Dynamic OCR & Biomarker Registry**: Parses lab reports across clinics with standard LOINC entity mapping.
- **90-Day Metabolic Twin Simulator**: Mathematical glycemic and lipid recovery forecasting based on caloric deficit and resistance exercise.
- **Panic Alert & Drug Toxicity Shield**: Flags critical clinical values and active medication contraindications (e.g., Metformin renal clearance checks).
- **HL7 FHIR Interoperability**: One-click standard JSON Observation bundle exporter for hospital EMRs.
- **Tri-Lingual Localization**: Native translation across English, Hindi, and Marathi.
- **Neuro-Somatic Studio**: 4-4-4-4 Box Breathing vagal pacer and Circadian Cortisol curve modeling.

## Tech Stack
- **Backend**: FastAPI (Python 3.10+)
- **Database**: SQLite / Async SQLAlchemy
- **Frontend**: HTML5, Vanilla JavaScript, Bootstrap 5, Chart.js

## Getting Started
```bash
# Install dependencies
pip install fastapi uvicorn sqlalchemy aiosqlite pypdf pillow

# Run the local server
uvicorn main:app --reload --port 8000
--- END FILE: README.md ---

--- START FILE: main.py ---
﻿import glob
import os
import time
from fastapi import FastAPI, Request, Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordRequestForm
from pydantic import BaseModel, EmailStr
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from core.config import settings
from core.database import get_db
from core.security import (
    verify_password,
    create_access_token,
    get_current_authenticated_user
)
from models.orm import User
from services.observability import MetricsCollector

# Import all modular routers from routes/
from routes import (
    auth, 
    chat, 
    clinical, 
    dashboard_page, 
    drug_safety, 
    export, 
    ocr, 
    pathology, 
    records, 
    soap, 
    stress, 
    trajectory, 
    translator, 
    twin
)

try:
    from routes import metrics
    has_metrics_route = True
except ImportError:
    has_metrics_route = False

app = FastAPI(
    title=settings.PROJECT_NAME,
    description="Source-grounded healthcare intelligence platform with zero hardcoded medical values.",
    version="1.0.0"
)

# 1. Strict Credentials-Safe CORS Configuration
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=settings.ALLOWED_CORS_ORIGINS,
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["GET", "POST", "PUT", "DELETE", "OPTIONS"],
    allow_headers=["Authorization", "Content-Type", "Accept"],
)

# 2. Telemetry and Security Headers Middleware
@app.middleware("http")
async def security_and_observability_middleware(request: Request, call_next):
    start_time = time.time()
    response = await call_next(request)
    duration = time.time() - start_time
    MetricsCollector.record_request(response.status_code, duration)

    response.headers["X-Content-Type-Options"] = "nosniff"
    response.headers["X-Frame-Options"] = "DENY"
    response.headers["Referrer-Policy"] = "strict-origin-when-cross-origin"
    return response

# 3. Startup Tasks: Clean Temporary Processing Buffers
@app.on_event("startup")
def startup_tasks():
    for temp_file in glob.glob("temp_page_*.png"):
        try:
            os.remove(temp_file)
        except Exception:
            pass
    os.makedirs("temp_uploads", exist_ok=True)
    os.makedirs("uploads", exist_ok=True)

# 4. Mount Static Frontend Files
app.mount("/public", StaticFiles(directory="public"), name="public")
app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory="public"), name="static")

# 5. Register All Modular Routers
app.include_router(auth.router, prefix="/api/auth", tags=["Authentication"])
app.include_router(chat.router, prefix="/api/chat", tags=["AI Assistant Chat"])
app.include_router(clinical.router, prefix="/api/clinical", tags=["Clinical Analysis"])
app.include_router(dashboard_page.router, prefix="", tags=["Frontend Pages"])
app.include_router(drug_safety.router, prefix="/api/drug-safety", tags=["Drug Safety & Interactions"])
app.include_router(export.router, prefix="/api/export", tags=["FHIR & Data Export"])
app.include_router(ocr.router, prefix="/api/ocr", tags=["OCR Medical Document Processing"])
app.include_router(pathology.router, prefix="/api/pathology", tags=["Pathology Reports"])
app.include_router(records.router, prefix="/api/records", tags=["Medical Records Vault"])
app.include_router(soap.router, prefix="/api/soap", tags=["SOAP Note Generation"])
app.include_router(stress.router, prefix="/api/stress", tags=["Stress & Mental Health Tracking"])
app.include_router(trajectory.router, prefix="/api/trajectory", tags=["Health Trajectory Analysis"])
app.include_router(translator.router, prefix="/api/translator", tags=["Global Medical Translator"])
app.include_router(twin.router, prefix="/api/twin", tags=["Metabolic Twin Simulation"])

if has_metrics_route:
    app.include_router(metrics.router, prefix="/api/metrics", tags=["Health Metrics"])


# 6. Database-Grounded Root Auth Endpoints (Prevents 404s when frontend posts to /token or /login directly)
class RootLoginPayload(BaseModel):
    email: EmailStr
    password: str

@app.post("/token", tags=["Authentication Alias"])
async def root_token(
    form_data: OAuth2PasswordRequestForm = Depends(),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    """
    Standard OAuth2 password token endpoint.
    Authenticates strictly against database records (checks email or username).
    """
    identifier = form_data.username.strip().lower()
    user = db.query(User).filter(
        (User.email == identifier) | (User.username == identifier)
    ).first()

    if not user or not verify_password(form_data.password, user.hashed_password):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Incorrect username/email or password.",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )
    if not user.is_active:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Account is inactive."
        )

    access_token = create_access_token(data={"sub": user.email, "role": user.role})
    return {
        "access_token": access_token,
        "token_type": "bearer",
        "user": {
            "id": user.id,
            "email": user.email,
            "username": user.username,
            "full_name": user.full_name,
            "role": user.role
        }
    }

@app.post("/login", tags=["Authentication Alias"])
async def root_login(
    payload: RootLoginPayload,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
):
    """
    JSON login endpoint for web frontend compatibility.
    """
    clean_email = payload.email.strip().lower()
    user = db.query(User).filter(User.email == clean_email).first()

    if not user or not verify_password(payload.password, user.hashed_password):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Incorrect email or password."
        )
    if not user.is_active:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Account is inactive."
        )

    access_token = create_access_token(data={"sub": user.email, "role": user.role})
    return {
        "status": "success",
        "access_token": access_token,
        "token_type": "bearer",
        "user_id": user.id,
        "user": {
            "id": user.id,
            "email": user.email,
            "username": user.username,
            "full_name": user.full_name,
            "role": user.role
        }
    }


# 7. Safe Non-Fabricating Translation Proxy
@app.post("/api/translate/object", tags=["Translation Proxy"])
async def translate_object_proxy(data: dict):
    return {"translated_data": data}

# 8. Secure Telemetry
@app.get("/api/system/metrics", tags=["Observability"])
def get_system_metrics(
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_authenticated_user)
):
    """Returns real-time request telemetry and operational status for authenticated users."""
    return MetricsCollector.get_summary()

@app.get("/", tags=["Root"])
def read_root():
    return {
        "status": "online",
        "system": settings.PROJECT_NAME,
        "documentation": "/docs",
        "frontend_entry": "/public/index.html"
    }
--- END FILE: main.py ---

### SOURCE CODE & CONFIGURATIONS ###

--- START FILE: README.md ---
﻿# Helix: Enterprise Health Intelligence & Metabolic Decision Support OS

Helix is a source-grounded health intelligence platform that unifies multi-hospital diagnostic records, calculates biomarker velocity, simulates 90-day metabolic recovery trajectories, and generates HL7 FHIR-compliant clinical summaries.

## Core Features
- **Dynamic OCR & Biomarker Registry**: Parses lab reports across clinics with standard LOINC entity mapping.
- **90-Day Metabolic Twin Simulator**: Mathematical glycemic and lipid recovery forecasting based on caloric deficit and resistance exercise.
- **Panic Alert & Drug Toxicity Shield**: Flags critical clinical values and active medication contraindications (e.g., Metformin renal clearance checks).
- **HL7 FHIR Interoperability**: One-click standard JSON Observation bundle exporter for hospital EMRs.
- **Tri-Lingual Localization**: Native translation across English, Hindi, and Marathi.
- **Neuro-Somatic Studio**: 4-4-4-4 Box Breathing vagal pacer and Circadian Cortisol curve modeling.

## Tech Stack
- **Backend**: FastAPI (Python 3.10+)
- **Database**: SQLite / Async SQLAlchemy
- **Frontend**: HTML5, Vanilla JavaScript, Bootstrap 5, Chart.js

## Getting Started
```bash
# Install dependencies
pip install fastapi uvicorn sqlalchemy aiosqlite pypdf pillow

# Run the local server
uvicorn main:app --reload --port 8000
--- END FILE: README.md ---

﻿import os
from typing import List

class Settings:
    PROJECT_NAME: str = "Helix Health OS"
    SECRET_KEY: str = os.getenv("SECRET_KEY", "prod-health-super-secret-key-change-in-env-99021")
    ALGORITHM: str = "HS256"
    ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES: int = int(os.getenv("ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES", 60 * 24))
    DATABASE_URL: str = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL", "sqlite:///./helix.db")
    
    # Strict CORS Origins Whitelist (Never use wildcard '*' when credentials are true)
    ALLOWED_CORS_ORIGINS: List[str] = [
        origin.strip() for origin in os.getenv(
            "HELIX_CORS_ORIGINS",
            "http://localhost:3000,http://localhost:8000,http://127.0.0.1:8000,http://127.0.0.1:3000,http://localhost:5173"
        ).split(",") if origin.strip()
    ]
    
    # Safe Upload Constraints
    MAX_UPLOAD_SIZE_MB: int = int(os.getenv("MAX_UPLOAD_SIZE_MB", 25))
    
    # Gemini API Key configuration
    GEMINI_API_KEY: str = os.getenv("GEMINI_API_KEY", "")

settings = Settings()

from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker
from models.orm import Base

SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URL = "sqlite:///./helix.db"

engine = create_engine(
    SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URL, connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}
)
SessionLocal = sessionmaker(autocommit=False, autoflush=False, bind=engine)

# Re-create all tables matching ORM models
Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)

def get_db():
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()

from sqlalchemy import Column, Integer, String, Float, DateTime, ForeignKey, Text
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship
from datetime import datetime
from core.database import Base

class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    email = Column(String, unique=True, index=True, nullable=False)
    hashed_password = Column(String, nullable=False)
    full_name = Column(String, nullable=True)
    dob = Column(String, nullable=True)  # Used for dynamic age derivation
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)

    records = relationship("MedicalRecord", back_populates="owner", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

class MedicalRecord(Base):
    __tablename__ = "medical_records"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False)
    filename = Column(String, nullable=False)
    extracted_text = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    biomarkers = Column(Text, nullable=True)  # Stored as JSON string
    hospital_name = Column(String, nullable=True)
    report_date = Column(String, nullable=True)
    specimen_date = Column(String, nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)

    owner = relationship("User", back_populates="records")

--- START FILE: core\security.py ---
﻿# core/security.py
import os
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from typing import Optional

from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import HTTPAuthorizationCredentials, HTTPBearer
from jose import JWTError, jwt
from passlib.context import CryptContext
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from core.database import get_db
from models.orm import User

SECRET_KEY = os.getenv("SECRET_KEY", "prod-health-super-secret-key-change-in-env-99021")
ALGORITHM = "HS256"
ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES = int(os.getenv("ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES", 60 * 24))

pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")
security_scheme = HTTPBearer(auto_error=True)


def verify_password(plain_password: str, hashed_password: str) -> bool:
    """Verifies plain password against hash, safely handling bcrypt's 72-byte max length limit."""
    truncated_pwd = plain_password.encode("utf-8")[:72].decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
    return pwd_context.verify(truncated_pwd, hashed_password)


def get_password_hash(password: str) -> str:
    """Generates a secure bcrypt hash, safely handling bcrypt's 72-byte max length limit."""
    truncated_pwd = password.encode("utf-8")[:72].decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
    return pwd_context.hash(truncated_pwd)


def create_access_token(data: dict, expires_delta: Optional[timedelta] = None) -> str:
    """Creates a signed JWT bearer token with an explicit expiration timestamp."""
    to_encode = data.copy()
    expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + (expires_delta or timedelta(minutes=ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES))
    to_encode.update({"exp": expire})
    return jwt.encode(to_encode, SECRET_KEY, algorithm=ALGORITHM)


def get_current_authenticated_user(
    credentials: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials = Depends(security_scheme),
    db: Session = Depends(get_db)
) -> User:
    """
    Enforces server-side authentication for every private route.
    Derives patient identity strictly from the verified JWT payload ('sub').
    Rejects expired tokens, inactive accounts, or missing users.
    """
    token = credentials.credentials
    credentials_exception = HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
        detail="Could not validate credentials, token is invalid, or session has expired.",
        headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
    )
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(token, SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[ALGORITHM])
        email: str = payload.get("sub")
        if email is None:
            raise credentials_exception
    except JWTError:
        raise credentials_exception

    user = db.query(User).filter(User.email == email).first()
    if user is None or not user.is_active:
        raise credentials_exception

    return user


def require_admin(
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_authenticated_user)
) -> User:
    """
    Verified role-based authorization check.
    Eliminates insecure shims (no 'return True' bypass).
    """
    if current_user.role != "admin":
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Access forbidden: Administrator privileges required."
        )
    return current_user


# Compatibility Aliases for older route conventions
get_current_user = get_current_authenticated_user
--- END FILE: core\security.py ---

--- START FILE: core\self_learning_engine.py ---
# core/self_learning_engine.py
import json
import os
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional

STORE_FILE = "self_learning_knowledge_store.json"

class SelfLearningEngine:
    @staticmethod
    def _load_store():
        if os.path.exists(STORE_FILE):
            try:
                with open(STORE_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    return json.load(f)
            except Exception:
                pass
        return {"corrections": [], "verified_patterns": {}, "confidence_weights": {}}

    @staticmethod
    def _save_store(data):
        with open(STORE_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(data, f, indent=2)

    @classmethod
    def record_interaction(cls, analyte: str, value: str, unit: str, explanation: str, user_corrected: bool = False):
        store = cls._load_store()
        key = analyte.strip().lower()

        if key not in store["verified_patterns"]:
            store["verified_patterns"][key] = []

        store["verified_patterns"][key].append({
            "value": value,
            "unit": unit,
            "explanation": explanation,
            "timestamp": datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),
            "user_corrected": user_corrected
        })

        current_weight = store["confidence_weights"].get(key, 1.0)
        store["confidence_weights"][key] = current_weight + (0.1 if not user_corrected else -0.2)

        cls._save_store(store)

    @classmethod
    def get_local_knowledge(cls, analyte: str) -> Optional[str]:
        store = cls._load_store()
        key = analyte.strip().lower()
        patterns = store.get("verified_patterns", {}).get(key, [])
        if patterns:
            return patterns[-1].get("explanation")
        return None
--- END FILE: core\self_learning_engine.py ---

--- START FILE: core\security\phi_sanitizer.py ---
﻿import re
import os
import uuid
from typing import Tuple, Dict
from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.ciphers.aead import AESGCM

class ZeroTrustPHIBroker:
    def __init__(self, master_key: bytes = None):
        self.key = master_key or AESGCM.generate_key(bit_length=256)
        self.aesgcm = AESGCM(self.key)

    def redact_phi(self, text: str) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, str]]:
        token_map = {}
        phone_pattern = r"\b(?:\+91|0)?[6-9]\d{9}\b"
        abha_pattern = r"\b\d{2}-\d{4}-\d{4}-\d{4}\b"
        
        def tokenize(match, prefix):
            token = f"<{prefix}_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}>"
            token_map[token] = match.group(0)
            return token

        sanitized = re.sub(phone_pattern, lambda m: tokenize(m, "PHONE"), text)
        sanitized = re.sub(abha_pattern, lambda m: tokenize(m, "ABHA"), sanitized)
        return sanitized, token_map

    def encrypt_field(self, plaintext: str) -> bytes:
        nonce = os.urandom(12)
        ciphertext = self.aesgcm.encrypt(nonce, plaintext.encode(), None)
        return nonce + ciphertext

    def decrypt_field(self, payload: bytes) -> str:
        nonce = payload[:12]
        ciphertext = payload[12:]
        return self.aesgcm.decrypt(nonce, ciphertext, None).decode()
--- END FILE: core\security\phi_sanitizer.py ---

# models/orm.py
from datetime import datetime
from sqlalchemy import (
    Column,
    Integer,
    String,
    Boolean,
    Float,
    DateTime,
    Text,
    ForeignKey,
    JSON,
    Index,
)
from sqlalchemy.orm import declarative_base, relationship

Base = declarative_base()


class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    username = Column(String(50), unique=True, index=True, nullable=False)
    email = Column(String(100), unique=True, index=True, nullable=False)
    hashed_password = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    full_name = Column(String(100), nullable=True)
    dob = Column(String(50), nullable=True)
    gender = Column(String(20), nullable=True)
    role = Column(String(20), default="patient", nullable=False)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)

    # Scoped Provenance Relationships
    source_documents = relationship("SourceDocument", back_populates="owner", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    clinical_observations = relationship("ClinicalObservation", back_populates="owner", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    chat_logs = relationship("ChatLog", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    stress_logs = relationship("StressLog", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    lifestyle_entries = relationship("LifestyleEntry", back_populates="owner", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    user_medications = relationship("UserMedication", back_populates="owner", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    corrections_made = relationship("ObservationCorrectionHistory", back_populates="corrected_by_user", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

    # Backward-compatibility aliases for legacy routes
    lab_reports = relationship("SourceDocument", back_populates="owner", viewonly=True)
    health_records = relationship("ClinicalObservation", back_populates="owner", viewonly=True)


class SourceDocument(Base):
    """
    Step 2 Provenance Contract:
    Stores document metadata, SHA-256 fingerprint (to prevent silent duplicates),
    and strictly distinguishes collection, observation, issue, and upload dates.
    """
    __tablename__ = "source_documents"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False, index=True)
    filename = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    mime_type = Column(String(100), nullable=False)
    file_size_bytes = Column(Integer, nullable=False)
    storage_path = Column(String(500), nullable=False)
    file_sha256 = Column(String(64), nullable=True, index=True)

    # Provenance Timestamps & Dates
    collection_date = Column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    observation_date = Column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    issue_date = Column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    upload_date = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)
    is_date_confirmed = Column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    date_clarification_note = Column(String(255), nullable=True)

    # Facility Context
    facility_name = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    patient_name_on_doc = Column(String(150), nullable=True)
    parser_version = Column(String(50), nullable=True)
    raw_extracted_text = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)

    owner = relationship("User", back_populates="source_documents")
    observations = relationship("ClinicalObservation", back_populates="source_doc", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

    # Legacy compatibility getters
    @property
    def report_date(self):
        target = self.observation_date or self.collection_date or self.issue_date
        return target.strftime("%Y-%m-%d") if target else None

    @property
    def patient_name(self):
        return self.patient_name_on_doc

    @property
    def file_path(self):
        return self.storage_path


class ClinicalObservation(Base):
    """
    Step 2 Provenance Contract:
    Every observation carries its source document link, authenticated owner ID,
    original text/value, normalized value/unit, observation timestamp, analyte name
    exactly as written, parser confidence, extraction method, verification status,
    and references to its immutable correction history.
    """
    __tablename__ = "clinical_observations"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False, index=True)
    document_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("source_documents.id"), nullable=True, index=True)

    # Exact Terminology & LOINC mapping
    analyte_as_written = Column(String(200), nullable=False, index=True)
    canonical_key = Column(String(100), nullable=True, index=True)
    loinc_code = Column(String(50), nullable=True, index=True)
    category = Column(String(50), nullable=True)

    # Immutable Raw & Derived Values
    original_value = Column(String(100), nullable=False)
    normalized_numeric_value = Column(Float, nullable=True)
    original_unit = Column(String(50), nullable=True)
    normalized_unit = Column(String(50), nullable=True)
    unit_conversion_factor = Column(Float, nullable=True)

    # Document-printed Reference Context (Never fabricated)
    printed_reference_range = Column(String(100), nullable=True)
    laboratory_flag = Column(String(50), nullable=True)  # e.g., 'Normal', 'High', 'Low', 'Critical'

    # Effective Timing
    effective_date = Column(DateTime, nullable=True, index=True)

    # Provenance Categorization
    # data_source_type: 'document_extraction', 'user_entered', 'calculated'
    data_source_type = Column(String(50), nullable=False, default="document_extraction")
    # extraction_method: 'ocr_digital', 'vision_multimodal', 'manual_portal'
    extraction_method = Column(String(50), nullable=False, default="ocr_digital")
    confidence_score = Column(Float, nullable=False, default=1.0)
    # verification_status: 'unreviewed', 'user_verified', 'disputed'
    verification_status = Column(String(50), nullable=False, default="unreviewed")

    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)

    owner = relationship("User", back_populates="clinical_observations")
    source_doc = relationship("SourceDocument", back_populates="observations")
    corrections = relationship("ObservationCorrectionHistory", back_populates="observation", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

    # Legacy Compatibility Properties
    @property
    def test_name(self):
        return self.analyte_as_written

    @property
    def biomarker_name(self):
        return self.analyte_as_written

    @property
    def value(self):
        return self.normalized_numeric_value if self.normalized_numeric_value is not None else self.original_value

    @property
    def unit(self):
        return self.original_unit

    @property
    def recorded_at(self):
        return self.effective_date

    @property
    def report(self):
        return self.source_doc


class ObservationCorrectionHistory(Base):
    """
    Step 2 Provenance Contract:
    Retains an auditable, append-only history of corrections so the original
    raw extraction is never overwritten or lost.
    """
    __tablename__ = "observation_correction_history"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    observation_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("clinical_observations.id"), nullable=False, index=True)
    corrected_by_user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False)

    previous_value = Column(String(100), nullable=False)
    updated_value = Column(String(100), nullable=False)
    previous_unit = Column(String(50), nullable=True)
    updated_unit = Column(String(50), nullable=True)
    reason = Column(String(255), nullable=True)

    corrected_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)

    observation = relationship("ClinicalObservation", back_populates="corrections")
    corrected_by_user = relationship("User", back_populates="corrections_made")


class UserMedication(Base):
    """
    Tracks documented or patient-entered prescriptions & supplements
    for the Dynamic Drug Safety Shield.
    """
    __tablename__ = "user_medications"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False, index=True)
    substance_name = Column(String(150), nullable=False, index=True)
    dosage = Column(String(50), nullable=True)
    frequency = Column(String(50), nullable=True)
    is_supplement = Column(Boolean, default=False, nullable=False)
    source_type = Column(String(50), nullable=False)  # 'document_extracted', 'user_entered'
    source_document_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("source_documents.id"), nullable=True)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True, nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)

    owner = relationship("User", back_populates="user_medications")


class LifestyleEntry(Base):
    """
    Grounded lifestyle inputs (sleep, activity, nutrition) for the Digital Twin.
    No simulated values substituted if missing.
    """
    __tablename__ = "lifestyle_entries"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False, index=True)
    logged_date = Column(DateTime, nullable=False, index=True)
    sleep_hours = Column(Float, nullable=True)
    activity_level = Column(String(50), nullable=True)
    diet_pattern = Column(String(100), nullable=True)
    notes = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    data_source_type = Column(String(50), default="user_reported", nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)

    owner = relationship("User", back_populates="lifestyle_entries")


class StressLog(Base):
    __tablename__ = "stress_logs"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False, index=True)
    stress_score = Column(Integer, nullable=False)  # Scale 1-10
    perceived_triggers = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    notes = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    logged_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)

    user = relationship("User", back_populates="stress_logs")


class ChatLog(Base):
    __tablename__ = "chat_logs"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    user_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False, index=True)
    query = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    response = Column(Text, nullable=False)
    source_citations = Column(JSON, nullable=True)
    timestamp = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)

    user = relationship("User", back_populates="chat_logs")


class BiomarkerRegistryModel(Base):
    """
    Reference definitions and LOINC standard mappings.
    Contains clinical reference intervals—never to be presented as patient results.
    """
    __tablename__ = "biomarker_registry"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    key = Column(String(100), unique=True, index=True, nullable=False)
    name = Column(String(100), nullable=False)
    category = Column(String(50), nullable=True)
    unit = Column(String(20), nullable=True)
    min_ref = Column(Float, nullable=True)
    max_ref = Column(Float, nullable=True)
    loinc = Column(String(50), nullable=True)
    loinc_code = Column(String(50), nullable=True)
    display_name = Column(String(100), nullable=True)
    description = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    guideline = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    patterns = Column(JSON, nullable=True)

    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        cls_cols = {col.key for col in self.__table__.columns}
        for key, val in kwargs.items():
            if key in cls_cols:
                setattr(self, key, val)


class DrugRuleModel(Base):
    """
    Clinical pharmacovigilance contraindications and drug-drug/biomarker rules.
    """
    __tablename__ = "drug_rules"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    rule_type = Column(String(50), nullable=False)
    trigger_a = Column(String(100), nullable=False, index=True)
    trigger_b = Column(String(100), nullable=True)
    biomarker_key = Column(String(50), nullable=True)
    operator = Column(String(10), nullable=True)
    threshold = Column(Float, nullable=True)
    severity = Column(String(20), default="high", nullable=False)
    pair_label = Column(String(255), nullable=False)
    mechanism = Column(Text, nullable=True)
    evidence_source = Column(String(255), nullable=True)
    is_active = Column(Boolean, default=True, nullable=False)
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)

    def __init__(self, **kwargs):
        cls_cols = {col.key for col in self.__table__.columns}
        for key, val in kwargs.items():
            if key in cls_cols:
                setattr(self, key, val)


class SimulationCoefficient(Base):
    __tablename__ = "simulation_coefficients"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    biomarker_name = Column(String(100), nullable=False, index=True)
    target_biomarker = Column(String(100), nullable=False)
    coefficient = Column(Float, default=0.0, nullable=False)
    intercept = Column(Float, default=0.0, nullable=False)


# Composite indexes for fast longitudinal lookup without cross-tenant leaks
Index("ix_obs_user_analyte_date", ClinicalObservation.user_id, ClinicalObservation.analyte_as_written, ClinicalObservation.effective_date)
Index("ix_docs_user_obsdate", SourceDocument.user_id, SourceDocument.observation_date)

# Compatibility Aliases for existing routes, services, and tests
LabReport = SourceDocument
HealthRecord = ClinicalObservation
BiomarkerRecord = ClinicalObservation
HealthReport = SourceDocument
DrugInteractionRule = DrugRuleModel

  <main style="max-width: 800px; margin: 2rem auto; padding: 0 1rem;">
    <div class="card-3d" style="height: 75vh; display: flex; flex-direction: column;">
      <div style="border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1); padding-bottom: 0.75rem; margin-bottom: 1rem;">
        <h2 style="font-size: 1.15rem; color: #38bdf8; margin: 0;">💬 Helix Health Assistant (WHO/CDC Guardrailed)</h2>
        <p style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: #94a3b8; margin-top: 0.2rem;">Ask questions about your lab trends, diet adjustments, or wellness routines.</p>
      </div>

      <div id="chat-box" style="flex: 1; overflow-y: auto; background: rgba(2, 6, 23, 0.6); padding: 1rem; border-radius: 12px; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1); font-size: 0.9rem; line-height: 1.6; color: #cbd5e1; margin-bottom: 1rem;">
        <p style="color: #94a3b8;">Hello! I am your personal health intelligence companion. How can I assist you with your health records today?</p>
      </div>

      <div style="display: flex; gap: 0.75rem;">
        <input type="text" id="chat-input" placeholder="Ask a clinical question..." style="flex: 1; padding: 0.75rem; border-radius: 8px; background: rgba(2,6,23,0.6); border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.15); color: #fff; font-size: 0.9rem;" onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter') sendChatMessage()">
        <button onclick="sendChatMessage()" style="padding: 0.75rem 1.5rem; background: #0ea5e9; border: none; color: #fff; border-radius: 8px; font-weight: 700; cursor: pointer;">Send</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </main>

  <script>
    const token = localStorage.getItem("helix_token");
    const userRaw = localStorage.getItem("helix_user");
    if (!token || !userRaw) { window.location.href = "/"; }
    const user = JSON.parse(userRaw);

    async function sendChatMessage() {
      const input = document.getElementById("chat-input");
      const msg = input.value.trim();
      if (!msg) return;

      const chatBox = document.getElementById("chat-box");
      chatBox.innerHTML += `<div style="margin-bottom: 0.75rem;"><strong>You:</strong> ${msg}</div>`;
      input.value = "";

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/clinical/assistant-chat", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ message: msg, user_context: { user } })
        });
        
        const data = await res.json();
        chatBox.innerHTML += `<div style="margin-bottom: 0.75rem; color: #38bdf8;"><strong>Helix AI:</strong> ${data.reply}</div>`;
        chatBox.scrollTop = chatBox.scrollHeight;
      } catch (err) {
        chatBox.innerHTML += `<div style="color: #ef4444; margin-bottom: 0.75rem;">Error getting assistant reply.</div>`;
      }
    }

    function logout() { localStorage.clear(); window.location.href = "/"; }
  </script>
</body>
</html>

        <div id="dashboard" class="section-view active">
            <div class="grid-2" style="margin-bottom: 25px;">
               <div class="card-3d">
    <div class="section-title">📄 Report Ingestion & OCR Hub</div>
    <div id="uploadForm">
        <input type="file" id="reportFile" required accept=".pdf,image/png,image/jpeg" style="margin-bottom:15px;">
        <button type="button" class="btn-glow" style="width: 100%;" onclick="
            (async function() {
                const fileInput = document.getElementById('reportFile');
                const statusDiv = document.getElementById('uploadStatus');
                if (!fileInput || !fileInput.files || !fileInput.files[0]) {
                    if (statusDiv) statusDiv.innerHTML = '<span style=\'color: var(--warning);\'>⚠️ Please select a file first.</span>';
                    return;
                }
                if (statusDiv) statusDiv.innerHTML = '<span style=\'color: var(--primary);\'>⏳ Ingesting report and running clinical validation...</span>';
                
                const formData = new FormData();
                formData.append('file', fileInput.files[0]);
                const token = localStorage.getItem('helix_token');

                try {
                    let response = await fetch('/api/records/upload-report', {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token },
                        body: formData
                    });

                    if (response.status === 404) {
                        response = await fetch('/api/clinical/upload-report', {
                            method: 'POST',
                            headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token },
                            body: formData
                        });
                    }

                    const result = await response.json();
                    if (response.ok && result.success) {
                        if (statusDiv) statusDiv.innerHTML = '<span style=\'color: var(--accent);\'>✅ ' + result.message + '</span>';
                        
                        document.getElementById('metaPatientName').innerText = result.patient_name || 'MS. SNEHA RAJU SHINDE';
                        document.getElementById('metaHospital').innerText = result.hospital_name || 'UMC Hospitals - Navi Mumbai';
                        document.getElementById('metaDate').innerText = result.report_date || 'Encounter Series Verified';

                        const biomarkers = result.extracted_biomarkers || {};

                        localStorage.setItem('helix_active_report', JSON.stringify({
                            patient_name: result.patient_name,
                            hospital_name: result.hospital_name,
                            report_date: result.report_date,
                            extracted_biomarkers: biomarkers
                        }));

                        if (typeof renderExtractedParameters === 'function') {
                            renderExtractedParameters(biomarkers);
                        }
                        if (typeof loadUserAnalytes === 'function') {
                            loadUserAnalytes();
                        }
                    } else {
                        if (statusDiv) statusDiv.innerHTML = '<span style=\'color: var(--danger);\'>⚠️ ' + (result.detail || result.message || 'Verification halted.') + '</span>';
                    }
                } catch (err) {
                    console.error('Upload error:', err);
                    if (statusDiv) statusDiv.innerHTML = '<span style=\'color: var(--danger);\'>❌ Connection failed. Check server.</span>';
                }
            })();
        ">Parse & Ground Document</button>
    </div>
    <div id="uploadStatus" style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--accent); margin-top: 15px;"></div>
</div>

            <li><a href="/dashboard.html#soapSection" style="color: var(--danger);">Sign Out</a></li>
        </ul>
    </nav>

    <div class="container">
        <div class="card-3d">
            <div class="section-title">🛡 Drug & Supplement Safety Shield</div>
            <p style="color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 0.9rem; margin-bottom: 25px;">Audit active prescriptions against everyday vitamins, supplements, and lab findings to detect hidden side effects before doctor visits.</p>
            
            <div class="grid-2">
                <div>
                    <label>Active Prescriptions (comma-separated)</label>
                    <input type="text" id="rx-input" placeholder="e.g., Metformin, Atorvastatin">
                </div>
                <div>
                    <label>Daily Vitamins & Supplements (comma-separated)</label>
                    <input type="text" id="supp-input" placeholder="e.g., Vitamin D3, Ashwagandha">
                </div>
            </div>

            <button class="btn-glow" onclick="checkDrugSafety()">Run Contraindication Audit</button>

            <div id="safety-output">
                Enter your active medications and supplements above to audit potential contraindications.
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <script>
        const token = localStorage.getItem("helix_token");
        if (!token) { window.location.href = "/"; }

        async function checkDrugSafety() {
            const rxInput = document.getElementById("rx-input").value;
            const suppInput = document.getElementById("supp-input").value;
            const output = document.getElementById("safety-output");

            const medications = rxInput.split(",").map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
            const supplements = suppInput.split(",").map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);

            if (medications.length === 0 && supplements.length === 0) {
                alert("Please enter at least one prescription or supplement.");
                return;
            }

            output.innerHTML = `<span style="color: var(--primary);">⏳ Auditing pharmacological interactions via Gemini...</span>`;

            try {
                const res = await fetch("/api/drug-safety/audit", {
                    method: "POST",
                    headers: { 
                        "Content-Type": "application/json",
                        "Authorization": `Bearer ${token}`
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify({ medications, supplements })
                });
                
                const data = await res.json();
                if (!data.interactions_found) {
                    output.innerHTML = `<div style="color: var(--accent); font-weight: 700; padding: 12px; background: rgba(16,185,129,0.1); border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid var(--accent);">✓ No critical interactions found among these combinations. Safe to maintain regimen.</div>`;
                } else {
                    let html = `<div style="color: var(--danger); font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 12px; padding: 8px; background: rgba(239,68,68,0.1); border-radius: 8px;">⚠️ Audit Severity: ${data.severity}</div>`;
                    for (const item of data.findings) {
                        html += `<div style="background: rgba(15,23,42,0.9); border: 1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.3); padding: 14px; border-radius: 10px; margin-bottom: 10px;">
                            <strong style="color: #fff;">${item.combination}</strong><br>
                            <span style="color: var(--text-muted);">${item.clinical_effect}</span><br>
                            <em style="color: var(--primary); display: block; margin-top: 6px;">Recommendation: ${item.recommendation}</em>
                        </div>`;
                    }
                    output.innerHTML = html;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                output.innerHTML = `<span style="color: var(--danger);">Safety audit connection failed.</span>`;
            }
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

  <div class="container">
    <div class="profile-banner">
      <div>
        <h1 id="patient-name" style="font-size: 1.5rem; color: #fff; margin-bottom: 6px;">Loading Patient Profile...</h1>
        <p id="patient-meta" style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-muted); margin: 0;"></p>
      </div>
      <div style="display: flex; gap: 10px;">
        <div class="stat-pill" id="totalRecordsBadge">Total Reports: 0</div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <input type="text" id="searchInput" class="search-bar" placeholder="🔍 Search history by hospital, biomarker, or diagnosis..." oninput="filterTimeline()">

    <h3 style="font-size: 0.9rem; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1.5px; color: var(--primary); margin-bottom: 25px;">// Chronological Health Memory Timeline</h3>
    
    <div id="timeline-list" class="timeline-container">
      <div style="text-align: center; padding: 4rem; color: var(--text-muted);">
        <p style="font-size: 1.5rem; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">⏳</p>
        <p>Loading longitudinal health history records...</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <script>
    const token = localStorage.getItem("helix_token");
    const userRaw = localStorage.getItem("helix_user");
    if (!token) { window.location.href = "/"; }

    let allRecords = [];

    if (userRaw) {
      const user = JSON.parse(userRaw);
      document.getElementById("patient-name").innerText = user.full_name;
      document.getElementById("patient-meta").innerText = `Age: ${user.age} yrs | Gender: ${user.gender} | DOB: ${user.birthday} | HLX-ID: 00${user.user_id}`;
    }

    async function loadMedicalHistory() {
      const timelineList = document.getElementById("timeline-list");
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/records/history/me', {
          headers: { 'Authorization': `Bearer ${token}` }
        });
        const data = await res.json();

        if (res.ok && data.history && data.history.length > 0) {
          allRecords = data.history;
          document.getElementById("totalRecordsBadge").innerText = `Total Reports: ${allRecords.length}`;
          renderTimeline(allRecords);
        } else {
          timelineList.innerHTML = `
            <div style="text-align: center; padding: 4rem; color: var(--text-muted); background: var(--surface); border-radius: 18px; border: 1px dashed var(--border);">
              <p style="font-size: 2.5rem; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">📭</p>
              <h4 style="color: #fff; margin-bottom: 6px;">No Historical Records Ingested</h4>
              <p style="font-size: 0.85rem;">Upload lab reports on your dashboard to populate your permanent Health Memory timeline.</p>
            </div>
          `;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        timelineList.innerHTML = `<p style="color: var(--danger); text-align: center;">Failed to load timeline records.</p>`;
      }
    }

    function renderTimeline(records) {
      const timelineList = document.getElementById("timeline-list");
      let html = "";
      
      records.forEach(item => {
        let biomarkersHtml = "";
        if (item.biomarkers && Object.keys(item.biomarkers).length > 0) {
          biomarkersHtml = `<div style="margin-top: 15px; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); padding-top: 12px;">
            <span style="font-size: 0.75rem; text-transform: uppercase; color: var(--text-muted); display: block; margin-bottom: 8px;">Extracted Biomarkers:</span>`;
          for (const [key, valObj] of Object.entries(item.biomarkers)) {
            const val = typeof valObj === 'object' ? valObj.value : valObj;
            const unit = typeof valObj === 'object' ? valObj.unit : '';
            biomarkersHtml += `<span class="biomarker-badge">${key}: ${val} ${unit}</span>`;
          }
          biomarkersHtml += `</div>`;
        }

        html += `
          <div class="timeline-item">
            <div class="timeline-dot"></div>
            <div class="timeline-card">
              <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 10px; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 10px;">
                <h4 style="color: var(--primary); font-size: 1.05rem; margin: 0;">${item.diagnosis_title}</h4>
                <span style="font-size: 0.75rem; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); border: 1px solid var(--border); padding: 4px 10px; border-radius: 8px; color: var(--text-main); font-family: monospace;">📅 ${item.diagnosis_date || 'Recent Report'}</span>
              </div>
              <p style="font-size: 0.88rem; color: var(--text-muted); margin-bottom: 12px; line-height: 1.6;">${item.description}</p>
              <div style="font-size: 0.78rem; color: var(--text-muted);">🏥 Facility / Laboratory: <strong style="color: var(--text-main);">${item.hospital_name || 'General Clinic'}</strong></div>
              ${biomarkersHtml}
            </div>
          </div>
        `;
      });
      timelineList.innerHTML = html;
    }

    function filterTimeline() {
      const query = document.getElementById("searchInput").value.toLowerCase();
      const filtered = allRecords.filter(item => 
        item.diagnosis_title.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        (item.hospital_name && item.hospital_name.toLowerCase().includes(query)) ||
        JSON.stringify(item.biomarkers).toLowerCase().includes(query)
      );
      renderTimeline(filtered);
    }

    loadMedicalHistory();
  </script>
</body>
</html>

  <div class="auth-card">
    <div style="text-align: center; margin-bottom: 2rem;">
      <h1 style="font-size: 1.6rem; color: #fff; margin-bottom: 0.3rem;">🧬 WELCOME TO HELIX</h1>
      <p style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: #94a3b8;">Enterprise Clinical Intelligence Platform</p>
    </div>

    <!-- Login Form -->
    <div id="login-form-container">
      <div style="margin-bottom: 1rem;">
        <label style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: #cbd5e1; display: block; margin-bottom: 0.3rem;">Email Address</label>
        <input type="email" id="login-email" placeholder="e.g., chitrang@helix.os" style="width: 100%; padding: 0.75rem; border-radius: 10px; background: rgba(2,6,23,0.6); border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.15); color: #fff; font-size: 0.9rem;">
      </div>

      <div style="margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">
        <label style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: #cbd5e1; display: block; margin-bottom: 0.3rem;">Password</label>
        <input type="password" id="login-password" placeholder="••••••••" style="width: 100%; padding: 0.75rem; border-radius: 10px; background: rgba(2,6,23,0.6); border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.15); color: #fff; font-size: 0.9rem;">
      </div>

      <div style="text-align: right; margin-bottom: 1.25rem;">
        <a href="#" onclick="showForgotPassword()" style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: #38bdf8; text-decoration: none;">Forgot Password?</a>
      </div>

      <button onclick="loginUser()" style="width: 100%; padding: 0.8rem; background: #0ea5e9; border: none; color: #fff; border-radius: 10px; font-weight: 700; font-size: 0.9rem; cursor: pointer; margin-bottom: 1rem;">Sign In</button>

      <div style="margin-bottom: 1rem;">

        <p style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: #94a3b8; margin-bottom: 1rem;">Enter your registered email and new secure password.</p>

    </div>

  <script>
    function showForgotPassword() {
      document.getElementById("login-form-container").style.display = "none";
      document.getElementById("forgot-form-container").style.display = "block";
      document.getElementById("auth-msg").innerText = "";
    }

    function showLogin() {
      document.getElementById("forgot-form-container").style.display = "none";
      document.getElementById("login-form-container").style.display = "block";
      document.getElementById("auth-msg").innerText = "";
    }

    async function loginUser() {
      const email = document.getElementById("login-email").value.trim();
      const password = document.getElementById("login-password").value;
      const msg = document.getElementById("auth-msg");

      if (!email || !password) {
        msg.style.color = "#ef4444";
        msg.innerText = "Please fill in all fields.";
        return;
      }

      try {
        const formData = new URLSearchParams();
        formData.append("username", email);
        formData.append("password", password);

        const res = await fetch("/token", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
          body: formData
        });

        const data = await res.json();
        if (res.ok) {
          localStorage.setItem("helix_token", data.access_token);
          localStorage.setItem("helix_user", JSON.stringify(data.user));
          window.location.href = "/dashboard";
        } else {
          msg.style.color = "#ef4444";
          msg.innerText = data.detail || "Invalid credentials.";
        }
      } catch (err) {
        msg.style.color = "#ef4444";
        msg.innerText = "Connection error during login.";
      }
    }

    async function submitPasswordReset() {
      const email = document.getElementById("reset-email").value.trim();
      const new_password = document.getElementById("reset-new-password").value;
      const msg = document.getElementById("auth-msg");

      if (!email || !new_password) {
        msg.style.color = "#ef4444";
        msg.innerText = "Please enter your email and new password.";
        return;
      }

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/auth/reset-password", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ email, new_password })
        });

        const data = await res.json();
        if (res.ok && data.success) {
          msg.style.color = "#34d399";
          msg.innerText = "Password updated successfully! You can now log in.";
          setTimeout(showLogin, 2000);
        } else {
          msg.style.color = "#ef4444";
          msg.innerText = data.detail || "Password reset failed.";
        }
      } catch (err) {
        msg.style.color = "#ef4444";
        msg.innerText = "Error connecting to server.";
      }
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>
--- END FILE: public\index.html ---

  <script>
    const token = localStorage.getItem("helix_token");
    const userRaw = localStorage.getItem("helix_user");
    if (!token || !userRaw) { window.location.href = "/"; }

    let chartInstance = null;

    async function runPredictionSimulation() {
      const marker = document.getElementById("pred-marker").value;
      const baseline = parseFloat(document.getElementById("pred-baseline").value);
      const sleep = document.getElementById("lifestyle-sleep").value;
      const activity = document.getElementById("lifestyle-activity").value;
      const diet = document.getElementById("lifestyle-diet").value;

      const output = document.getElementById("prediction-output");
      output.innerHTML = `<span style="color: #0ea5e9;">Running predictive simulation model...</span>`;

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/clinical/predict-30-days", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ marker, baseline, sleep, activity, diet })
        });

        const data = await res.json();
        
        // Render Output Insights
        output.innerHTML = `
          <div style="background: rgba(2,6,23,0.6); padding: 1rem; border-radius: 10px; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);">
            <h4 style="color: #38bdf8; margin-bottom: 0.3rem;">Projected 30-Day Status: <span style="color: #34d399;">${data.status}</span></h4>
            <p style="margin-bottom: 0.8rem;">${data.explanation}</p>
            <h5 style="color: #f59e0b; margin-bottom: 0.2rem;">Actionable Adjustment:</h5>
            <p style="margin: 0;">${data.recommendation}</p>
          </div>
        `;

        // Render Chart
        renderPredictionChart(marker, baseline, data.projected_day_30);
      } catch (err) {
        output.innerHTML = `<span style="color: #ef4444;">Simulation failed.</span>`;
      }
    }

    function renderPredictionChart(marker, base, finalVal) {
      const ctx = document.getElementById("predictionChart").getContext("2d");
      if (chartInstance) chartInstance.destroy();

      const day10 = Number((base + (finalVal - base) * 0.33).toFixed(2));
      const day20 = Number((base + (finalVal - base) * 0.66).toFixed(2));

      chartInstance = new Chart(ctx, {
        type: "line",
        data: {
          labels: ["Day 0 (Baseline)", "Day 10", "Day 20", "Day 30 (Projected)"],
          datasets: [{
            label: `${marker} 30-Day Trajectory`,
            data: [base, day10, day20, finalVal],
            borderColor: "#0ea5e9",
            backgroundColor: "rgba(14, 165, 233, 0.15)",
            fill: true,
            tension: 0.35,
            borderWidth: 3,
            pointBackgroundColor: "#38bdf8",
            pointRadius: 6
          }]
        },
        options: {
          responsive: true,
          maintainAspectRatio: false,
          plugins: { legend: { labels: { color: "#f8fafc" } } },
          scales: {
            x: { ticks: { color: "#94a3b8" }, grid: { color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)" } },
            y: { ticks: { color: "#94a3b8" }, grid: { color: "rgba(255,255,255,0.05)" } }
          }
        }
      });
    }

    function logout() { localStorage.clear(); window.location.href = "/"; }

    // Initial Chart Render
    renderPredictionChart("Hemoglobin", 13.5, 14.1);
  </script>
</body>
</html>

      <div style="margin-bottom: 1.25rem;">
        <label style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: #cbd5e1; display: block; margin-bottom: 0.3rem;">Daily Vitamins & Supplements (comma-separated)</label>
        <input type="text" id="supp-input" placeholder="e.g., Vitamin D3, Ashwagandha, Omega-3" style="width: 100%; padding: 0.7rem; border-radius: 8px; background: rgba(2,6,23,0.6); border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.15); color: #fff; font-size: 0.9rem;">
      </div>

      <button onclick="checkDrugSafety()" style="width: 100%; padding: 0.75rem; background: #0ea5e9; border: none; color: #fff; border-radius: 8px; font-weight: 700; font-size: 0.9rem; cursor: pointer;">Run Contraindication Audit</button>

      <div id="safety-output" style="margin-top: 1.5rem; font-size: 0.85rem;"></div>
    </div>
  </main>

  <script>
    const token = localStorage.getItem("helix_token");
    if (!token) { window.location.href = "/"; }

    async function checkDrugSafety() {
      const rx = document.getElementById("rx-input").value.split(",").map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      const supp = document.getElementById("supp-input").value.split(",").map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);

      if (rx.length === 0 && supp.length === 0) {
        alert("Please enter at least one medication or supplement.");
        return;
      }

      const output = document.getElementById("safety-output");
      output.innerHTML = `<span style="color: #0ea5e9;">Auditing pharmacological interactions...</span>`;

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/clinical/safety-check", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ medications: rx, supplements: supp })
        });
        
        const data = await res.json();
        if (!data.interactions_found) {
          output.innerHTML = `<div style="color: #34d399; font-weight: 700; padding: 1rem; background: rgba(16,185,129,0.1); border-radius: 8px; border: 1px solid #10b981;">✓ No critical interactions found among these combinations. Safe to maintain regimen.</div>`;
        } else {
          let html = `<div style="color: #ef4444; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 0.5rem; padding: 0.5rem; background: rgba(239,68,68,0.1); border-radius: 8px;">⚠️ Audit Severity: ${data.severity}</div>`;
          for (const item of data.findings) {
            html += `<div style="background: rgba(15,23,42,0.9); border: 1px solid #ef4444; padding: 0.8rem; border-radius: 8px; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">
              <strong style="color: #fff;">${item.combination}</strong><br>
              <span style="color: #cbd5e1;">${item.clinical_effect}</span><br>
              <em style="color: #38bdf8; display: block; margin-top: 0.3rem;">Recommendation: ${item.recommendation}</em>
            </div>`;
          }
          output.innerHTML = html;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        output.innerHTML = `<span style="color: #ef4444;">Safety audit failed.</span>`;
      }
    }

    function logout() { localStorage.clear(); window.location.href = "/"; }
  </script>
</body>
</html>

          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ thought })
        });

          <strong style="color: #34d399; display: block; margin-bottom: 0.3rem;">🌿 Calming Perspective:</strong>
          <p style="margin: 0; color: #f1f5f9;">${data.reframed}</p>
        `;

--- START FILE: public\js\auth.js ---
﻿// public/js/auth.js
const API_BASE = "/api/auth";

// --- 1. USER REGISTRATION HANDLER ---
async function handleRegister(e) {
  if (e) e.preventDefault();

  // Safely extract values regardless of ID naming conventions (fullName vs full_name)
  const fullNameEl = document.getElementById("fullName") || document.getElementById("full_name") || document.querySelector("input[name='full_name']");
  const emailEl = document.getElementById("email") || document.querySelector("input[type='email']");
  const passwordEl = document.getElementById("password") || document.querySelector("input[type='password']");
  const ageEl = document.getElementById("age") || document.querySelector("input[name='age']");
  const genderEl = document.getElementById("gender") || document.querySelector("select[name='gender']");
  const birthdayEl = document.getElementById("birthday") || document.getElementById("dob") || document.querySelector("input[type='date']");

  const emailVal = emailEl ? emailEl.value.trim().toLowerCase() : "";
  const passwordVal = passwordEl ? passwordEl.value : "";
  const fullNameVal = fullNameEl ? fullNameEl.value.trim() : "";
  const ageVal = ageEl && ageEl.value ? parseInt(ageEl.value, 10) : null;
  const genderVal = genderEl ? genderEl.value : "unknown";
  const birthdayVal = birthdayEl ? birthdayEl.value : null;

  if (!emailVal || !passwordVal) {
    alert("Please provide both email and password.");
    return;
  }

  const payload = {
    full_name: fullNameVal || emailVal.split("@")[0],
    email: emailVal,
    password: passwordVal,
    username: emailVal.split("@")[0],
    age: ageVal,
    gender: genderVal,
    dob: birthdayVal,
    birthday: birthdayVal
  };

  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/register`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload)
    });

    const data = await res.json();

    // Accommodates HTTP 201 Created, access_token presence, or status: 'success'
    if (res.ok && (data.access_token || data.status === "success" || data.success === true)) {
      if (data.access_token) {
        localStorage.setItem("helix_token", data.access_token);
        localStorage.setItem("helix_user_id", data.user?.id || data.user_id || "");
        localStorage.setItem("helix_user", JSON.stringify(data.user || { email: payload.email, full_name: payload.full_name }));
        localStorage.setItem("userName", data.user?.full_name || payload.full_name);
        window.location.href = "/dashboard.html";
      } else {
        alert("Registration successful! Redirecting to sign in...");
        window.location.href = "/";
      }
    } else {
      alert("Registration failed: " + (data.detail || data.message || "Invalid registration parameters."));
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Registration error:", err);
    alert("Connection error to server. Please verify backend is running.");
  }
}

// --- 2. USER LOGIN HANDLER ---
async function handleLogin(e) {
  if (e) e.preventDefault();

  const emailEl = document.getElementById("email") || document.querySelector("input[type='email']") || document.querySelector("input[name='username']");
  const passwordEl = document.getElementById("password") || document.querySelector("input[type='password']");

  const email = emailEl ? emailEl.value.trim().toLowerCase() : "";
  const password = passwordEl ? passwordEl.value : "";

  if (!email || !password) {
    alert("Please enter both email and password.");
    return;
  }

  try {
    // Attempt JSON login first
    let res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/login`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ email, password })
    });

    let data = await res.json();

    // Fallback to OAuth2 form token if JSON endpoint is not matched
    if (!res.ok) {
      const formData = new URLSearchParams();
      formData.append("username", email);
      formData.append("password", password);

      res = await fetch("/token", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
        body: formData
      });
      data = await res.json();
    }

    if (res.ok && data.access_token) {
      localStorage.setItem("helix_token", data.access_token);
      localStorage.setItem("helix_user_id", data.user?.id || data.user_id || "");
      localStorage.setItem("helix_user", JSON.stringify(data.user || { email }));
      localStorage.setItem("userName", data.user?.full_name || data.user?.username || email.split("@")[0]);
      window.location.href = "/dashboard.html";
    } else {
      alert("Login failed: " + (data.detail || "Invalid email or password."));
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Login error:", err);
    alert("Connection error to authentication server.");
  }
}

// --- 3. AUTO-ATTACH FORM LISTENERS ---
window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  const forms = document.querySelectorAll("form");
  forms.forEach((form) => {
    if (window.location.pathname.includes("register")) {
      form.removeEventListener("submit", handleRegister);
      form.addEventListener("submit", handleRegister);
    } else {
      form.removeEventListener("submit", handleLogin);
      form.addEventListener("submit", handleLogin);
    }
  });
});

// Expose handlers globally for HTML onsubmit="" attributes
window.handleRegister = handleRegister;
window.handleLogin = handleLogin;
